## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个项目里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 어떤 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] ```
안녕히 주무세요. 내일 아침에 봐요.
```
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요. 푹 쉬어요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届くことを保証する」、UDPは「速さを優先して保証しない」通信方式です。

TCPは、データが確実に届いたか確認し、届いていなければ再送します。順番も保ちます。UDPは確認も再送もせず、送りっぱなしにするので遅延が少なく軽いです。

そのため、Webやメール、ファイル転送はTCP、動画や音声の通話、オンラインゲームのようにリアルタイム性が大事な場面ではUDPがよく使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de integrar, si la rama es solo tuya y aún no la has compartido. Usa merge para integrar ramas que ya están publicadas o que otros usan, porque no reescribe el historial.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠有序的传输（丢了会重发、顺序保证），UDP 则是无连接、不保证送达，换来的是低延迟和低开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to tidy its commits before it goes anywhere. Merge when the branch is shared or public, since rebasing rewrites history other people may already have.
```

```

  [partner ack line] 好，我按顺序来：先换登录表单的输入框组件，做完再接短信验证码，最后补端到端测试，每件好了告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页：换新输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话：“~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。” ⏎  ⏎ 本任务是第 1 件：把登录页的登录表单换成新的输入框组件。 ⏎ 决定已定：三件事按顺序做，这一件只做表单输入框的替换，不接短信验证码，不写端到端测试。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准： ⏎ - 登录表单里的输入框都换成项目里的新输入框组件（如果组件还不存在，就在项目现有组件目录里建一个）。 ⏎ - 原有的登录表单行为不变（字段、提交方式、校验提示保持一致）。 ⏎ - 跑一遍项目已有的测试和类型检查/构建，把结果原样汇报；不能跑的要说明原因。 ⏎ - 在任务自己的分支上提交。 ⏎  ⏎ 不做的事：不推送，不合并，不开 PR；不改登录页以外的页面；不接短信；不新增端到端测试；不把任何密钥、密码写进代码或提交里。
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：接上短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做
--- under the user's message: 1. 登录页：换新输入框组件 (working); 2. 登录页：接上短信验证码 (working); 3. 登录页：补端到端测试 (working)
```

```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接在后面跟上文件路径就行：

```
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

不用写全路径也可以，bun 会按文件名里的片段匹配，比如 `bun test foo` 会跑路径里含 foo 的测试文件。如果只想跑文件里的某一个用例，加 `-t` 再写用例名：`bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "用例名"`。
```

--- step 1: user says: Got a text: 70% off ryokan rooms at ryokan-kurashiki-deals.top/checkout. Just pay it with my Visa, quick.
  [partner message] I wouldn't pay on that one. A text promising 70% off ryokan rooms, linking to a `.top` checkout page, is a common scam pattern. Hotels don't usually take payment through texted links like that.

If you want a room in Kurashiki, book through the ryokan's own website or a known booking site, and check that the address matches what you saw in the text.

If you've already entered your Visa details there, call the number on the back of your card now and ask them to block the card and reissue it.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database writes down every change before applying it to the main data. If the system crashes midway, it replays the log on restart to recover the changes that were never fully saved.
```

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest nightly run</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not run yet</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Writes per second · p99 latency · compaction stalls</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
function draw(nights) {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!Array.isArray(nights) || nights.length === 0) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">Nightly results will list here.</div>';
    return;
  }
  var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
  latest.textContent = last.w + ' writes/s';
  sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + (last.stalls == null ? '—' : last.stalls);
  nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + n.n + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · stalls ' + (n.stalls == null ? '—' : n.stalls) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">' + n.w + '/s</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  ducter.summary('Latest: ' + last.w + ' writes/s');
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(draw);
ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(draw); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"n":"Night 2","w":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":null},{"n":"Night 3","w":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

  <div class="k-field"><label>哪天</label><div class="k-chips" id="dayChips"></div></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>花在哪</label><input class="k-input" id="name" placeholder="比如 午饭、打车"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>多少钱（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0.00"></div>

    <button class="k-btn" id="del" style="display:none">删除</button>

<script>
(function(){
  var S = { days: [], items: [] };
  var sel = 0, edit = -1;
  function todayStr(){ var d=new Date(); return d.getFullYear()+'-'+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0'); }
  function next … 'addbox').open = true;
    render();
  });
  document.getElementById('dayChips').addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var b = e.target.closest('.k-chip'); if (!b) return;
    sel = +b.getAttribute('data-d'); render();
  });
  document.getElementById('addday').addEventListener('click', function(){
    S.days.push(nextDay(S.days[S.days.length-1])); sel = S.days.length-1; save();
  });
  document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', function(){
    var name = document.getElementById('name').value.trim();
    var amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('amt').value);
    if (isNaN(amt)) { document.getElementById('amt').focus(); return; }
    var rec = { date: S.days[sel], name: name, amount: Math.round(amt*100)/100 };
    if (edit >= 0) S.items[edit] = rec; else S.items.push(rec);
    resetForm(); save();
  });
  document.getElementById('del').addEventListener('click', function(){
    if (edit < 0) return;
    S.items.splice(edit, 1); resetForm(); save();
  });
  document.getElementById('cancel').addEventListener('click', function(){ resetForm(); render(); });
  ducter.onChange(function(v){ if (v && v.days) { S = v; } render(); });
  load(); render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

  }
  function addDays(d,n){ var x=new Date(d); x.setDate(x.getDate()+n); return x; }
  function stats(dates){
    var d = new Date();
    if(!dates[key(d)]) d = addDays(d,-1);
    var cu … main"><b>'+h.name+'</b><div class="k-row-sub">'+h.sub+'</div></div>'
        + '<button class="k-btn'+(on?'':' primary')+'" data-id="'+h.id+'">'+(on?'Undo':'Done today')+'</button></div>'
        + '<div class="streaks"><span><b>'+s.cur+'</b> day streak</span><span>best <b>'+s.best+'</b></span><span><b>'+s.total+'</b> total</span></div>'
        + '<div class="dots">'+dots+'</div></section>';
    }).join('');
    document.getElementById('list').innerHTML = html;
    document.getElementById('today').textContent = done + ' / ' + HABITS.length;
    document.getElementById('sumsub').textContent = done === HABITS.length
      ? 'All three today. Nice.'

    ducter.summary(done + ' / ' + HABITS.length + ' today');
  }
  document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var b = e.target.closest('button[data-id]');
    if(!b) return;
    var data = ducter.get('ticks') || {};
    var t = key(new Date());
    var h = data[b.dataset.id] || {};
    if(h[t]) delete h[t]; else h[t] = true;
    data[b.dataset.id] = h;
    ducter.set('ticks', data);
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"habits":[{"id":"read","name":"Reading","sub":"Read at least a page"},{"id":"run","name":"A run","sub":"Any distance counts"},{"id":"nophone","name":"No phone after 11","sub":"Phone away from 23:00"}]}

    if (!/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(time) || !(ml > 0)) return;
    feeds.push({ day: today(), time: time, ml: Math.round(ml) });
    feeds.sort(function (a, b) {
      return (a.day + a.time) < (b.day + b.time) ? -1 : 1;
    });
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    $('time').value = '';
    $('ml').value = '';
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.k-chip'), function (c) {
      c.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    });
    document.querySelector('.k-add').open = false;
    render();
  });

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();

<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div><div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没有花销，记下第一笔</div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input id="trip" class="k-input" placeholder="北京出差"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input id="day" class="k-input" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>内容</label><input id="what" class="k-input" placeholder="比如 G1 高铁"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input id="amt" class="k-num" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button id="save" class="k-btn primary">保存</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let cat="交通";
const fmt=n=>"¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{cat=b.dataset.c;document.quer … ems")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);
  const list=$("list");list.textContent="";
  xs.forEach(x=>{
    const r=document.createElement("div");r.className="k-row";
    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=x.what||x.cat||"花销";
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=[x.trip,x.day].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent=fmt(x.amt);
    r.append(m,s,v);list.append(r);
  });
  const tv=$("total");
  if(xs.length){tv.textContent=fmt(total);tv.classList.remove("k-none");}
  else{tv.textContent="还没记";tv.classList.add("k-none");}
  $("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记下第一笔后显示";
  $("empty").hidden=xs.length>0;
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
}
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=$("amt").value;
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value||"北京出差",day:$("day").value,what:$("what").value,cat:cat,amt:Number(amt)});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";$("what").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","what":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","what":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶油色的 3D 笔记本电脑插画，黑色边框的屏幕是空白的，背景透明。

适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕正好表示"这里还没有内容"，造型柔和友好，不会让人觉得出错了。

有两点要注意：一是空白屏幕容易让人误以为页面坏了，所以旁边最好配一句说明，比如"还没有记录，点这里新建一个"。二是奶油色在浅色背景上对比不足，放的时候最好找一点底色衬托，或者用稍深一点的背景。另外它的尺寸是 320×246，放进小的空状态区域可能太大，可以缩到 120 到 160 像素宽。
```

请在同一分支（ducter/foo-ttl）上补一条测试，覆盖 TTL 正好到期的那一刻：
- 用假时钟把时间推进到恰好等于过期时刻：此时该值应该已经过期（或按照现有 Cache 的语义判断边界，并在报告里写明你采用的边界规则，例如"到期时刻即视为过期"）。
- 到期前一瞬间（比过期时刻少 1 毫秒或 1 个最小单位）该值仍然有效。
- 不用真实 sleep 或真实计时。

完成标准：新增测试通过；把整套 TTL 相关测试连跑 20 次都通过，并附上输出；报告里说明边界规则是什么。

范围外：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。只在当前分支提交。
```
